  constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Positions of all bodies are replicated on every rank as {x,y,z} triples so\n// that they can be exchanged with a single Allgatherv; velocities are only\n// stored for the locally owned slice.\nusing Coords = std::vector<double>;\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the local bodies [localOffset, localOffset+localN)\n// from all n bodies and advances the local velocities.\nstatic void computeForcesLocal(const double* __restrict pos, double* __restrict vel, const size_t n,\n                               const size_t localOffset, const size_t localN) {\n    size_t i = 0;\n\n    for (; i + BLOCK <= localN; i += BLOCK) {\n        double xi[BLOCK], yi[BLOCK], zi[BLOCK];\n        double Fx[BLOCK] = {}, Fy[BLOCK] = {}, Fz[BLOCK] = {};\n\n        for (size_t b = 0; b < BLOCK; ++b) {\n            xi[b] = pos[3 * (localOffset + i + b) + 0];\n            yi[b] = pos[3 * (localOffset + i + b) + 1];\n            zi[b] = pos[3 * (localOffset + i + b) + 2];\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (size_t b = 0; b < BLOCK; ++b) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[b];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[b];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[b];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[b] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[b] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[b] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t b = 0; b < BLOCK; ++b) {\n            vel[3 * (i + b) + 0] += DT * Fx[b];\n            vel[3 * (i + b) + 1] += DT * Fy[b];\n            vel[3 * (i + b) + 2] += DT * Fz[b];\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (; i < localN; ++i) {\n        const double xi = pos[3 * (localOffset + i) + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * (localOffset + i) + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * (localOffset + i) + 2];\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        vel[3 * i + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * i + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * i + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Advances the positions of the locally owned bodies into the local send buffer.\nstatic void integrateBodiesLocal(const double* __restrict pos, const double* __restrict vel,\n                                 double* __restrict posOut, const size_t localOffset, const size_t localN) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < 3 * localN; ++i) {\n        posOut[i] = pos[3 * localOffset + i] + vel[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const size_t n = numBodies > 0 ? static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) : 0;\n\n    // Block-distribute the bodies over the ranks; every rank derives the same\n    // decomposition and initial state, so no initial communication is needed.\n    std::vector<int> posCounts(numRanks), posDispls(numRanks);\n    for (int r = 0; \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n        \n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n            \n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        \n        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;\n        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;\n        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;\n        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;\n        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    \n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + \n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + \n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n    \n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        \n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse Positions are replicated on every rank (a single `{x,y,z}` triple array), velocities are stored only for the locally owned slice. Initialization is recomputed redundantly on each rank from the same seed, so there's no startup communication.\n\n**Per step**: each rank computes forces for its own bodies against all N (the O(N²) work, perfectly parallel), integrates its own positions, then a single `MPI_Allgatherv` of 3·N_local doubles republishes positions. One collective per step, O(N) bytes — negligible against O(N²/P) compute.\n\n**Force kernel** — restructured to process `BLOCK = 4` target bodies simultaneously with the source loop inner and sequential. This lets the compiler vectorize across the block lanes (4 sqrt/divides at once on AVX2) while each body's force accumulation keeps the original summation order. Worth ~2.4× on its own: single-rank is 7451 ms vs 29594 ms for the original serial code at n=20000, s=10.\n\n**Rank-count independence** — the decomposition is done in units of `BLOCK` bodies, so each rank's slice is aligned to the kernel blocking. Results are bit-identical for any number of ranks (verified for np = 1, 2, 3, 8, 17, 64).\n\nTiming takes the max across ranks after a barrier; only rank 0 prints. Final state is gathered to rank 0 for `-r` output and `-v` validation, whose energy computation stays in the original serial order; the exit code is broadcast so all ranks agree.\n\n## Correctness\n\nAgainst the unmodified serial binary at n=2003, s=7: Min/Max and several samples match bit-for-bit, the rest agree to ~1e-15 relative, and `Final energy` is identical. The residual difference is FMA-contraction placement, not algorithm — compiling both with `-ffp-contract=off` gives identical hashes. For n ≤ 7 the hashes match the reference exactly.\n\n## Scaling (n=20000, s=10, single node, 128 cores)\n\n| ranks | 1 | 2 | 4 | 8 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| ms | 7451 | 3735 | 1869 | 937 | 474 | 266 | 152 | 103 |\n\nNear-linear to 64 ranks (49×), 72× at 128.\n\n`nbody/CMakeLists.txt` adds `find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`; the executable name and existing flags are unchanged. No new files remain in the tree (I removed the scratch `build/` directory I used for testing)."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":345,"cache_read_input_tokens":44946,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":345},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"57ffa0f2-b834-40cc-9855-d52e53b41833","uuid":"433b13e6-4f71-4714-976b-27c5482bc75c","timestamp":"2026-09-12T13:52:45.923Z","request_id":"req_011CeyezrBiXzTofTpGbHTLD"}
